<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://bsuh.bearblog.dev/agents-need-control-flow/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Agents need control flow, not more prompts</span><span class='zh'>代理需要控制流，而非更多提示</span></a><span class="dom">bsuh.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Prompt-based thinking has plateaued for coordinating multi-step AI agents.</span><span class='zh'>用提示词来指挥多步代理的局限性逐渐显现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Prompt-based thinking has plateaued for coordinating multi-step AI agents. The piece argues that explicit control flow, state machines, and orchestration offer reliability and debuggability that prompts can&#x27;t. As AI agents tackle more complex workflows, control-flow design becomes essential.</span><span class='zh'>用提示词来指挥多步代理的局限性逐渐显现。文章主张通过显式控制流、状态机和任务编排来提升可靠性与可调试性；当代理处理更复杂的工作流时，控制流设计显得至关重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.techspot.com/news/112410-security-researcher-microsoft-secretly-built-backdoor-bitlocker-releases.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Security researcher says Microsoft built a Bitlocker backdoor, releases exploit</span><span class='zh'>安全研究员称微软在 BitLocker 中内置后门并公开漏洞利用代码</span></a><span class="dom">techspot.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security researcher claims Microsoft secretly built a BitLocker backdoor and has released an exploit.</span><span class='zh'>一名安全研究员声称微软在 BitLocker 中秘密内置后门并公开了利用代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security researcher claims Microsoft secretly built a BitLocker backdoor and has released an exploit. If true, it raises serious questions about encryption trust, potential covert access, and the need for independent audits of enterprise Windows components. The disclosure underscores why robust patch accountability and verifiable security assurances matter for widespread disk encryption.</span><span class='zh'>一名安全研究员声称微软在 BitLocker 中秘密内置后门并公开了利用代码。若属实，将引发对磁盘加密信任、潜在隐蔽访问和对企业 Windows 组件进行独立审计的必要性等重大疑问。此事凸显对修补程序可追溯性和可验证安全性的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chrismorgan.info/no-query-strings" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I’ve banned query strings</span><span class='zh'>我已禁止查询字符串</span></a><span class="dom">chrismorgan.info</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer bans query strings to improve cacheability and determinism in URLs.</span><span class='zh'>开发者宣布禁用查询字符串以提升 URL 的缓存性和确定性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer bans query strings to improve cacheability and determinism in URLs. This approach reduces cache fragmentation and enables more aggressive CDN caching, though it requires rethinking how parameters and analytics are handled. The post shares practical steps and trade-offs from their experience.</span><span class='zh'>开发者宣布禁用查询字符串以提升 URL 的缓存性和确定性。这一做法减少了缓存碎片，使 CDN 能更有效地缓存资源，但需要重新考虑参数和分析信息的处理方式。文中分享了基于其经验的实践要点与取舍。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Radley Balko recounts Garry Tan&#x27;s accusation that his reporting was unethical, setting up a rare public clash between a startup founder and a journalist.</span><span class='zh'>Radley Balko 描述了 YC CEO Garry Tan 指控其报道不道德的对峙，揭示创始人与记者之间罕见的公开冲突。</span></p>

It argues for transparent standards and robust verification in tech journalism.</span><span class='zh'>Radley Balko 描述了 YC CEO Garry Tan 指控其报道不道德的对峙，揭示创始人与记者之间罕见的公开冲突。此事凸显权力关系如何影响科技报道的边界与问责。强调在科技新闻中坚持透明标准与充分核实的重要性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">138</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thetypicalset.com/blog/thoughts-on-coding-agents" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bottleneck was never the code</span><span class='zh'>瓶颈从来不是代码</span></a><span class="dom">thetypicalset.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article argues that the real bottleneck in building effective AI agents isn’t code but the orchestration, data quality, and decision-making loops that feed them.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为构建高效 AI 代理的瓶颈并非代码本身，而是编排、数据质量与决策循环等要素。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article argues that the real bottleneck in building effective AI agents isn’t code but the orchestration, data quality, and decision-making loops that feed them. It stresses that tooling, memory, and integration with real-world data often limit performance more than algorithms. Takeaway: invest in systems design and data pipelines, not just faster code.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为构建高效 AI 代理的瓶颈并非代码本身，而是编排、数据质量与决策循环等要素。强调工具链、内存管理以及与真实数据的集成往往比算法本身更能决定成效。要点是：应更多投资于系统设计和数据管线，而非单纯追求更快的代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/05/21/six-search-engines-worth-trying-now-that-google-isnt-really-google-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Search engines alternatives now that Google isn&#x27;t Google anymore</span><span class='zh'>谷歌不再是谷歌：值得尝试的替代搜索引擎</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">148</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://idiallo.com/blog/ai-didnt-delete-your-database-you-did" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI didn&#x27;t delete your database, you did</span><span class='zh'>AI 没有删除你的数据库，是你删的</span></a><span class="dom">idiallo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that data loss often results from human error or misconfigurations rather than AI mishaps, calling for stronger backups, robust data governance, and clearer ownership of data workflows.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为数据丢失往往源自人为错误或配置失误，而非 AI 的失误，呼吁加强备份、健全的数据治理以及对数据工作流的明确责任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that data loss often results from human error or misconfigurations rather than AI mishaps, calling for stronger backups, robust data governance, and clearer ownership of data workflows. It emphasizes responsibility on operators and teams rather than blaming tooling.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为数据丢失往往源自人为错误或配置失误，而非 AI 的失误，呼吁加强备份、健全的数据治理以及对数据工作流的明确责任。强调将责任落到运营团队而非单纯指责工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://susam.net/inverse-laws-of-robotics.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Three Inverse Laws of AI</span><span class='zh'>AI 的三条逆向律</span></a><span class="dom">susam.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Three inverse laws of AI propose that as AI grows more capable, governance should tighten; interpretability remains essential for trust; and expectations should be tempered by practical limits.</span><span class='zh'>提出三条 AI 的逆向法则：随着能力提升，治理需要加紧；可解释性对建立信任至关重要；对现实边界应持保留态度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Three inverse laws of AI propose that as AI grows more capable, governance should tighten; interpretability remains essential for trust; and expectations should be tempered by practical limits. The piece uses humorous framing to underscore real-world constraints in safety and deployment. It invites readers to rethink AI governance alongside capability gains.</span><span class='zh'>提出三条 AI 的逆向法则：随着能力提升，治理需要加紧；可解释性对建立信任至关重要；对现实边界应持保留态度。以诙谐的笔法强调了现实世界在安全与落地中的约束，鼓励在能力增长的同时重新审视治理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://audiomass.co/?multitrack=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Audiomass – a free, open-source multitrack audio editor for the web</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Audiomass——一个免费开源的网页多轨音频编辑器</span></a><span class="dom">audiomass.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Audiomass is a free, open-source multitrack audio editor that runs entirely in your browser.</span><span class='zh'>Audiomass 是一个免费、开源的浏览器端多轨音频编辑器，完全在浏览器中运行，无需安装即可完成编辑与导出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Audiomass is a free, open-source multitrack audio editor that runs entirely in your browser. It offers a simple timeline and essential editing tools with no installation, making quick editing and sharing easy for web users. Being open-source, it invites community contributions and transparency.</span><span class='zh'>Audiomass 是一个免费、开源的浏览器端多轨音频编辑器，完全在浏览器中运行，无需安装即可完成编辑与导出。它提供简单的时间线和基本编辑工具，便于快速处理和分享音频，社区贡献也更易参与。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/anthropic-acquires-stainless" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic acquires Stainless</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 收购 Stainless</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has acquired Stainless, a startup focused on safety tooling and governance capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 收购 Stainless，一家专注于安全工具与治理能力的初创公司。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has acquired Stainless, a startup focused on safety tooling and governance capabilities. The deal signals Anthropic’s push to broaden enterprise-facing safety features as AI deployments scale. Integration could accelerate productization for customers and strengthen governance tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 收购 Stainless，一家专注于安全工具与治理能力的初创公司。此举昭示 Anthropic 将进一步扩展面向企业的安全功能，以应对大规模 AI 部署的合规与风险管理需求。并购有望加速对外提供的产品落地，并强化治理能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.construction-physics.com/p/how-an-oil-refinery-works" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How an oil refinery works</span><span class='zh'>炼油厂如何运作</span></a><span class="dom">construction-physics.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An oil refinery uses distillation towers to separate crude oil into fuels and feedstocks, then refines these streams through cracking, reforming, and blending.</span><span class='zh'>炼油厂通过塔式蒸馏等工艺把原油分离成燃料和化工原料，再经裂解、改质和混合进行深加工。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An oil refinery uses distillation towers to separate crude oil into fuels and feedstocks, then refines these streams through cracking, reforming, and blending. The process is large-scale, energy-intensive, and central to energy economics, making understanding it valuable for anyone following infrastructure tech.</span><span class='zh'>炼油厂通过塔式蒸馏等工艺把原油分离成燃料和化工原料，再经裂解、改质和混合进行深加工。这个过程规模庞大、能耗高，是能源经济的核心，理解其流程有助于把握能源市场的运行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rubbishtalk.com/economy/how-private-equity-bought-americas-essential-services/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Private equity bought America&#x27;s essential services</span><span class='zh'>私募股权买下美国的基础公共服务</span></a><span class="dom">rubbishtalk.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that private equity has increasingly acquired essential public-facing services, raising concerns about debt loads, cost-cutting, and long-term resilience.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出私募股权日益收购关键公共服务，带来债务压力、成本削减和长期韧性方面的担忧，可能影响可负担性与可及性，同时揭示资本密集型行业中的激励机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that private equity has increasingly acquired essential public-facing services, raising concerns about debt loads, cost-cutting, and long-term resilience. It highlights potential risks to affordability and access while outlining the incentives at play in capital-heavy sectors.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出私募股权日益收购关键公共服务，带来债务压力、成本削减和长期韧性方面的担忧，可能影响可负担性与可及性，同时揭示资本密集型行业中的激励机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</span><span class='zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</span></a><span class="dom">thesciverse.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta is at odds after reports that employees who witnessed colleagues using smart glasses to observe sexual activity were fired. The situation highlights tensions around wearable surveillance, employee reporting, and HR policies in a highly visible tech company.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Meta 团队中一些在目睹同事通过智能眼镜观看性行为后被解雇的员工，引发了关于穿戴式监控、举报流程与人力资源政策的争议。这一事件凸显了科技巨头在员工安全与隐私之间的博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://davidoks.blog/p/ai-is-killing-the-cheap-smartphone" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The memory shortage is causing a repricing of consumer electronics</span><span class='zh'>内存短缺正在重塑消费电子价格</span></a><span class="dom">davidoks.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Global memory shortages are driving up the cost of consumer electronics as DRAM and NAND demand climbs for AI and data-center workloads.</span><span class='zh'>全球内存短缺推高了消费电子的成本，随着 AI 和数据中心对 DRAM 与 NAND 的需求上升，厂商将更高的元件成本传导至便宜设备，推动智能手机等产品的价格再定价。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Global memory shortages are driving up the cost of consumer electronics as DRAM and NAND demand climbs for AI and data-center workloads. Vendors pass higher component costs to budget devices, accelerating price repricing across smartphones and other devices. The shift reflects broader supply-chain dynamics in semiconductors.</span><span class='zh'>全球内存短缺推高了消费电子的成本，随着 AI 和数据中心对 DRAM 与 NAND 的需求上升，厂商将更高的元件成本传导至便宜设备，推动智能手机等产品的价格再定价。这一变化反映了半导体供应链的更广泛动态。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/darrylmorley/whatcable" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WhatCable, a tiny menu bar app for inspecting USB-C cables</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：WhatCable——用于查看 USB-C 电缆的小型菜单栏应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WhatCable is a tiny macOS menu-bar app that inspects USB-C cables to report capabilities like power delivery and alt-mode support.</span><span class='zh'>WhatCable 是一个极简的 macOS 菜单栏应用，可检查 USB-C 电缆的供电、替代模式等能力，帮助开发者快速核对硬件兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WhatCable is a tiny macOS menu-bar app that inspects USB-C cables to report capabilities like power delivery and alt-mode support. It demonstrates how lightweight utilities can help developers verify hardware compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>WhatCable 是一个极简的 macOS 菜单栏应用，可检查 USB-C 电缆的供电、替代模式等能力，帮助开发者快速核对硬件兼容性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.democrata.es/en/politics/congress-and-senate/congress-will-act-against-massive-ip-blockages-by-laliga/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spain&#x27;s parliament will act against massive IP blockages by LaLiga</span><span class='zh'>西班牙议会将对 LaLiga 的大规模 IP 阻断采取行动</span></a><span class="dom">democrata.es</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spain&#x27;s parliament plans to curb mass IP blockades pushed by La Liga, addressing concerns over overbroad blocks that can affect legitimate sites.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙议会计划限制 La Liga 推动的广泛 IP 阻断措施，纠正对合法站点和服务的过度封锁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spain&#x27;s parliament plans to curb mass IP blockades pushed by La Liga, addressing concerns over overbroad blocks that can affect legitimate sites. The move signals a push for more measured, privacy-respecting enforcement while preserving anti-piracy aims. It highlights the tension between content protection and civil liberties in digital policy.</span><span class='zh'>西班牙议会计划限制 La Liga 推动的广泛 IP 阻断措施，纠正对合法站点和服务的过度封锁。此举旨在在实现反盗版目标的同时，推动更克制、尊重隐私的执法方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/grand-theft-oil-futures" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Grand Theft Oil Futures: Insider traders keep making a killing at our expense</span><span class='zh'>巨额油价期货内幕交易者继续坐收利润</span></a><span class="dom">paulkrugman.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Paul Krugman argues that insiders keep profiting from oil futures, extracting wealth at the public&#x27;s expense due to market structure and regulatory gaps.</span><span class='zh'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，油价期货市场的内幕交易者持续从中获利，市场结构和监管漏洞使公众承受成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Paul Krugman argues that insiders keep profiting from oil futures, extracting wealth at the public&#x27;s expense due to market structure and regulatory gaps. The essay highlights how information advantages and opaque pricing enable rent extraction, and it calls for policy remedies to curb excess profits.</span><span class='zh'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，油价期货市场的内幕交易者持续从中获利，市场结构和监管漏洞使公众承受成本。文章揭示信息优势与定价不透明如何促成利润攫取，并呼吁通过政策改革来遏制这类过度获利。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/delivering-low-latency-voice-ai-at-scale/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How OpenAI delivers low-latency voice AI at scale</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 如何在大规模部署中实现低延迟语音 AI</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI discusses delivering low-latency voice AI at scale, detailing architectural choices, streaming inference, and optimization strategies that keep real-time speech apps responsive.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 介绍在大规模场景下实现低延迟语音 AI 的设计思路，涵盖流式推理、优化策略及跨规模部署的要点，为构建可扩展的实时语音应用提供实用洞见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI discusses delivering low-latency voice AI at scale, detailing architectural choices, streaming inference, and optimization strategies that keep real-time speech apps responsive. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://lenz.io/research/llm-disagreement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Disagreement among frontier LLMs on real-world fact-checks</span><span class='zh'>前沿大模型在现实世界事实核查中的分歧</span></a><span class="dom">lenz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study shows frontier LLMs disagree on factual claims in real-world fact-check tasks, underscoring reliability limits and evaluation challenges.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示前沿大模型在现实世界的事实核查中对同一事实往往持不同结论，暴露出可靠性与评估难题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study shows frontier LLMs disagree on factual claims in real-world fact-check tasks, underscoring reliability limits and evaluation challenges. The findings suggest need for ensemble methods, calibration, and human-in-the-loop to prevent misinformation in critical applications.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示前沿大模型在现实世界的事实核查中对同一事实往往持不同结论，暴露出可靠性与评估难题。结果强调需要多模型集成、校准，以及人工在环来降低关键应用中的误导风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://meshtastic.org/docs/introduction/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Introduction to Meshtastic</span><span class='zh'>Meshtastic 入门指南</span></a><span class="dom">meshtastic.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meshtastic is an open-source mesh-networking project that enables off-grid text messaging using low-power radios like LoRa.</span><span class='zh'>Meshtastic 是一个开源网状通信项目，利用低功耗无线电（如 LoRa）在无蜂窝网络的情况下实现短讯传输。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meshtastic is an open-source mesh-networking project that enables off-grid text messaging using low-power radios like LoRa. This introduction covers core concepts, components, and practical use cases, plus trade-offs in latency and range compared with cellular networks.</span><span class='zh'>Meshtastic 是一个开源网状通信项目，利用低功耗无线电（如 LoRa）在无蜂窝网络的情况下实现短讯传输。本文概述了核心概念、组成部分和典型用例，并讨论了与蜂窝网络相比的延迟和覆盖范围权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.autonocion.com/us/tesla-lithium-refinery-texas/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery discharges 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater a day</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每日排放约231,000加仑污染废水</span></a><span class="dom">autonocion.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery in Texas is discharging about 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater daily, drawing environmental and regulatory scrutiny.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每天排放约231,000加仑污染废水，引发环境和监管关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla&#x27;s lithium refinery in Texas is discharging about 231,000 gallons of polluted wastewater daily, drawing environmental and regulatory scrutiny. The figure underscores the water-management challenges that accompany rapid EV supply-chain growth and could affect local communities and ecosystems. For engineers and policymakers, it highlights the need for robust wastewater treatment and transparent environmental reporting for new battery facilities.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉在德州的锂精炼厂每天排放约231,000加仑污染废水，引发环境和监管关注。这一数字凸显在迅速扩张的电动车供应链中水资源管理的挑战，可能影响周边社区与生态系统。对工程师和决策者而言，这强调需要对新建电池厂进行严格的废水处理与透明的环境披露。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://archestra.ai/blog/only-responsible-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We stopped AI bot spam in our GitHub repo using Git&#x27;s –author flag</span><span class='zh'>我们用 Git 的 --author 标志阻止了仓库里的机器人垃圾信息</span></a><span class="dom">archestra.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>By enforcing commit authorship with Git&#x27;s --author flag, the team significantly cut spam from automated bots in their repo.</span><span class='zh'>通过在提交时强制使用 Git 的 --author 标志来标识作者，该团队显著降低了仓库中的机器人垃圾信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>By enforcing commit authorship with Git&#x27;s --author flag, the team significantly cut spam from automated bots in their repo. The approach blocks impersonation at the source and complements existing security controls. It shows how simple version-control metadata can curb abuse without heavy ML defenses.</span><span class='zh'>通过在提交时强制使用 Git 的 --author 标志来标识作者，该团队显著降低了仓库中的机器人垃圾信息。此做法在源头阻断冒充行为，辅以其他安全控制。该案例证明了版本控制元数据在反滪用方面的实用性，不必依赖复杂的机器学习防护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260513-your-car-is-spying-on-you-its-about-to-get-worse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cars collect a startling amount of data about you</span><span class='zh'>汽车收集的数据量令人震惊</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Modern cars collect far more data than drivers realize—from location to driving habits and preferences—raising privacy concerns as cars become more connected.</span><span class='zh'>现代汽车收集的数据远超驾驶者的认知范围，包括位置信息、驾驶习惯和偏好等，这在车联网日益普及的背景下引发隐私担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Modern cars collect far more data than drivers realize—from location to driving habits and preferences—raising privacy concerns as cars become more connected.</span><span class='zh'>现代汽车收集的数据远超驾驶者的认知范围，包括位置信息、驾驶习惯和偏好等，这在车联网日益普及的背景下引发隐私担忧。文章探讨了数据的收集、访问方以及未来可能的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/trq212/status/2052809885763747935" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Using Claude Code: The unreasonable effectiveness of HTML</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude Code：HTML 的非同寻常高效</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code demonstrates that HTML&#x27;s simple, deterministic structure can yield reliable, quick wins for AI-assisted frontend tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 展示了 HTML 的简单、确定性结构在 AI 辅助前端任务中能带来可靠且快速的收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code demonstrates that HTML&#x27;s simple, deterministic structure can yield reliable, quick wins for AI-assisted frontend tasks. HTML&#x27;s straightforward nature makes it a practical starting point for AI-driven UI prototyping and code generation. This highlights HTML as a durable substrate for AI copilots in frontend work.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 展示了 HTML 的简单、确定性结构在 AI 辅助前端任务中能带来可靠且快速的收益。HTML 的直接性使其成为 AI 驱动的 UI 原型和代码生成的实际起点。此现象凸显了 HTML 在前端工作中作为 AI 协作工具的持久价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/apr/30/ai-outperforms-doctors-in-harvard-trial-of-emergency-triage-diagnoses" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI’s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients vs. 50-55% by triage doctors</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的 o1 在急诊诊断中正确率达 67%，超出分诊医生的 50–55%</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In a Harvard trial, OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients, compared with 50-55% by triage doctors.</span><span class='zh'>在哈佛的一项试验中，o1 在急诊患者诊断中的正确率达到 67%，明显高于分诊医生的 50–55%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In a Harvard trial, OpenAI&#x27;s o1 correctly diagnosed 67% of ER patients, compared with 50-55% by triage doctors. This demonstrates AI&#x27;s potential to augment emergency triage, but real-world deployment must address safety, workflow integration, and clinician oversight.</span><span class='zh'>在哈佛的一项试验中，o1 在急诊患者诊断中的正确率达到 67%，明显高于分诊医生的 50–55%。这显示人工智能有潜力提升急诊分诊的效率与准确性，但将其落地需解决安全性、工作流整合以及医生监督等挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Blue Origin&#x27;s New Glenn rocket exploded during a ground static-fire test, marking a major setback for the program.</span><span class='zh'>Blue Origin 的 New Glenn 在一次地面静态点火测试中爆炸，这是对该计划的重大挫折。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Blue Origin&#x27;s New Glenn rocket exploded during a ground static-fire test, marking a major setback for the program. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI’s WebRTC challenges highlight the friction between real-time media needs and browser/network realities in AI-powered web apps.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题揭示 AI 驱动网页应用中实时媒体需求与浏览器/网络现实之间的摩擦。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI’s WebRTC challenges highlight the friction between real-time media needs and browser/network realities in AI-powered web apps. The issues point to where WebRTC adoption stalls and what developers might optimize—latency, security, and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的 WebRTC 问题揭示 AI 驱动网页应用中实时媒体需求与浏览器/网络现实之间的摩擦。相关问题指向需要优化的领域，如延迟、安全性与兼容性，以及在实际应用中的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://reflex.dev/blog/computer-use-is-45x-more-expensive-than-structured-apis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Computer Use is 45x more expensive than structured APIs</span><span class='zh'>计算使用成本比结构化 API 高出 45 倍</span></a><span class="dom">reflex.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Computing for unstructured tasks can be 45x more expensive than using structured APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一项分析称，处理非结构化任务的计算成本大约比使用结构化 API 高出 45 倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Computing for unstructured tasks can be 45x more expensive than using structured APIs. This highlights the cost benefits of API-based tooling and disciplined data orchestration for AI workloads. It nudges engineers to design pipelines around reliable interfaces rather than ad-hoc compute.</span><span class='zh'>一项分析称，处理非结构化任务的计算成本大约比使用结构化 API 高出 45 倍。这凸显了基于 API 的工作流在 AI 负载中的成本优势，促使开发者以稳定接口而非随意计算来设计数据处理管线。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thran.uk/writ/hdid/2025/12/simcity-3k-in-4k.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SimCity 3k in 4k (2025)</span><span class='zh'>SimCity 3000 4K 重制（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">thran.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A fan-made project recreates SimCity 3000 in 4K, updating visuals while preserving the classic city-building gameplay.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩家自制项目将 SimCity 3000 重制为 4K，保留经典玩法的同时提升画质。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A fan-made project recreates SimCity 3000 in 4K, updating visuals while preserving the classic city-building gameplay. The effort highlights how modern upscaling, art direction, and emulation can breathe new life into beloved 2000s games. For retro enthusiasts and streamers, it signals a growing niche of high-res nostalgia experiences.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩家自制项目将 SimCity 3000 重制为 4K，保留经典玩法的同时提升画质。此举展示了现代放大、美术风格更新与仿真技术如何为经典游戏注入新活力。对于怀旧玩家和主播而言，预示着高分辨率的复古体验日益兴起。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>What we lose when we stop letting kids leave the front yard argues that outdoor exploration builds resilience and independence, and that safety-first culture can curb curiosity.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为让孩子在户外探索、承担风险有助于培养韧性与独立性，过度的安全文化可能削弱好奇心。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nair.sh/guides-and-opinions/communicating-your-expertise/why-senior-developers-fail-to-communicate-their-expertise" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why senior developers fail to communicate their expertise</span><span class='zh'>资深开发者为何难以传达专业能力</span></a><span class="dom">nair.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Senior developers often fail to translate deep expertise into accessible guidance.</span><span class='zh'>资深开发者常常难以将深厚的专业知识转化为易于理解的指导。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Senior developers often fail to translate deep expertise into accessible guidance. The piece identifies gaps in storytelling, problem framing, and sharing decision processes, not just hard skills. It offers practical tactics like narrating workflows, giving reproducible examples, and clearly outlining tradeoffs.</span><span class='zh'>资深开发者常常难以将深厚的专业知识转化为易于理解的指导。文章指出在讲故事、问题表述和分享决策过程等方面存在断层，而不仅仅是技能本身的问题，并给出通过讲解工作流、提供可复现示例以及清晰陈述权衡取舍等具体做法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://koenvangilst.nl/lab/mistral-ai-now-summit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Notes from the Mistral AI Now Summit</span><span class='zh'>巴黎 Mistral AI Now Summit 现场笔记</span></a><span class="dom">koenvangilst.nl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Paris hosted the Mistral AI Now Summit with announcements on open-weight models, partnerships, and product roadmaps.</span><span class='zh'>巴黎举行的 Mistral AI Now Summit 现场聚焦开放权重模型、伙伴关系和产品路线图。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Paris hosted the Mistral AI Now Summit with announcements on open-weight models, partnerships, and product roadmaps. The notes capture what leaders envision for European AI tooling and governance in a rapidly evolving ecosystem. This matters for developers building open models and for investors watching AI infrastructure competition.</span><span class='zh'>巴黎举行的 Mistral AI Now Summit 现场聚焦开放权重模型、伙伴关系和产品路线图。笔记记录了领导者对欧盟AI工具链与治理的展望，以及快速发展的生态带来的影响。对构建开放模型的开发者和关注AI基础设施竞争的投资者都具重要意义。</span></p>
